Projection onto simplex: need efficient top-level function:
def proj_simplex(v):
  v = np.sort(v)[::-1]; n=len; cssv = cumsum(v)-1; cond=v-cssv>0; I=cond.sum(); theta=v[I]-cssv[I]/I; u=np.maximum(v-theta,0); if u.sum()!=1 adjust? Standard.
But v length 7000, each step.

- If k-j == j (k=2j): new contribution (v²) vs a_j², change v² - old_j².

Design PF iteration:
def pf_flatten(a, iters=200, gamma=1.0, target='inverse'):
  a=normalize
  for it:
    q=fast_conv(a); M=q.max()
    # target t: weight low lags more. Use t = 1/(q+eps)^(gamma), or t = exp(-γ q / M). Need sum 1.
    if target='inverse': t = 1/(q + eps) ; pow gamma maybe.
    t /= t.sum()
    # PF step: r = operator(t, a); r might be large. We want a_new ∝ (1-α)a + α r/r.sum()? Since r = B_t a, PF eigenvector. Normalize.
    s = r.sum()
    if s>0: a_new = (1-alpha)*a + alpha*(r/s)
    normalize.
    score; track best.
  return best
This is a linear iteration; may converge to PF of changing t. Could help fill valleys. Use gamma sweep [0.5,1,2]. But t sum not 1? D. L-BFGS on z. This likely key. Let's detail objective_z.

def softmax(x):
  m = np.max(x)
  y = np.exp(x-m)
  s = y.sum()
  if s <=0 or not finite: y=np.ones_like(x); s=n
  return y/s

objective_z(x, beta):
  a = softmax(x)
  q=fast_conv(a)
  M=q.max()
  z = beta*(q-M)
  # p = exp(z - maxz)/sum; maxz=0
  expz = np.exp(z) # since z<=0; may underflow fine
  p=expz; psum=p.sum()
  if psum<=0: p top uniform? If beta huge and only max? At q=M exponent 0 -> exp=1; psum≥1.
  L = M + np.log(psum)/beta
  # r = operator(p,a) (p not normalized? derivative of L uses weights exp β(q-M)/sum. We can normalize p after.
  p = p / psum
  r = op(p, a)
  grad_a = 4.0 * n * r  # since f=2n L, ∂L=2r
  grad_z = a * (grad_a - np.sum(a*grad_a))
  return 2.0*n*L, grad_z

Need ensure n = len(x) global? objective has x length; compute n = len(x).

L-BFGS memory 20, maxiter? We can call minimize with jac=True, options ftol, gtol, maxfun. Since objective smooth. But z has scale flat; we can initialize z = np.log(a0+1e-12) - mean(log). Maybe add small bounds? L-BFGS-B unbounded. We can impose bounds (-50,50) maybe; but if true a very small, log may be < -50? Values ≥? def z_fun(x, beta):
```
    n = len(x)
    x_clipped? maybe no.
    xc = np.clip(x, -40, 40)  # to avoid overflow; gradient discontinuity? We can make soft clip? Use np.tanh? Better not. Softmax stable with large x no overflow (subtract max), but z values huge make a one-hot, derivative zero. L-BFGS may step to huge. Add penalty on z². Clip at 60 for safety; gradient zero at clip, not ideal but prevents overflow. Use xc = np.where(x>60,60,...). At boundaries, gradient not exact; but maybe fine.
    a = softmax(xc)
    ...
    f = 2*n*L
    grad_a = 4*n*r
    # natural gradient
    mean = np.dot(a, grad_a)
    grad = a*(grad_a - mean)
    # penalty
    if penalty>0: grad += pen*xc; f += 0.5*pen*np.dot(xc,xc)
    return f, grad
```

```
def lbfgs_on_z(a, beta, maxfun, maxiter):
  a=normalize(a)
  z=np.log(a+1e-12); z-=z.mean()
  bounds=None
  res=minimize(z_fun, z, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-10,'maxls':20})
  a_new=softmax(res.x - res.x.mean())
  return a_new
```

```
def coordinate_descend(a, sweeps=3, top=300, cand_list=None):
    a=normalize_seq(a); q=fast_conv(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_from_q
    L=len(q); n=len(a)
    factors = [0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,0.9,0.95,1.0,1.05,1.1,1.2,1.4,1.8,2.5]
    for sweep in range(sweeps):
       q=fast_conv(a)
       pref = np.maximum.accumulate(q); suff=...
       M=q.max(); sc=2nM; update best
       # coord_score
       cs = np.zeros(n)
       top_lags = np.argpartition(q, -min(50,L))[-min(50,L):]
       for k in top_lags:
          if q[k] <= 0.85*M: # maybe still top
          js=np.arange(max(0,k-n+1), min(k,n-1)+1)
          # contributions a[js]*a[k-js]
          cs[js] += (q[k]/M)**2 * a[js]*a[k-js]
       # add random/ top? Select top `top` coords
       if top < n: idxs=np.argpartition(cs, -top)[-top:]
       else idxs=np.arange(n)
       # maybe random 50
       rng? coordinate func deterministic? Use global rng? We can pass seed or use np.random. We can create local rng = np.random.default_rng(123) because top-level? okay.
       for j in idxs:
          if time optional break within? Need top-level no closures, but can accept time_limit parameter. We'll check outside maybe.
          # prefix/suffix for current q
          pref = np.maximum.accumulate(q); suff = np.maximum.accumulate(q[::-1])[::-1]
          start=j; end=j+n
          comp = max(pref[start-1] if start>0 else 0, suff[end] if end<L else 0)
          o=a[j]
          cand_base = o*np.array(factors)
          # include maybe exact target zeros: 0, small eps
          cand = np.unique(np.concatenate((cand_base, np.array([0.0, 1e-6, np.sqrt(max(comp,0))]))))
          cand = np.clip(cand,0,1000)
          Snew = 1 - o + cand
          valid = Snew > 1e-8
          if not any continue
          c = cand - o
          scale = 1/(Snew*Snew)
          # affected max
          qr = q[start:end]
          aff = qr[None,:] + 2.0*c[:,None]*a[None,:]  # uses old a
          aff[:, j] += c*c  # if j within 0..n-1 yes
          max_aff = aff.max(axis=1) * scale[valid?]
          max_aff[~valid]=inf
          max_all = np.maximum(max_aff, comp*scale)
          scv = 2*n*max_all
          valid_idx=np.where(valid & (scv < best_local - 1e-15))[0]
          if len>0: choose min; accept.
       # periodic recompute q
       q=fast_conv(a)
    return best_a
```

Fienup Frequency implementation maybe:
```
def fienup_power(a, iters=200):
  n=len(a); L=2n-1; M_fft=next_pow2(L)
  a=normalize; best...
  for it:
    alpha=0.1+0.5*it/iters
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, M_fft)
    P=np.abs(A)**2
    P_mean=np.mean(P)
    P_blend=(1-alpha)*P + alpha*P_mean
    P_blend=np.maximum(P_blend,1e-30)
    scale=np.sqrt(P_blend/P)
    scale=np.clip(scale, 0.1, 10.0) # new amplitude magnitude = |A|*scale
    A_new=A*scale
    a_new=np.fft.irfft(A_new, M_fft)[:n]
    a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0)
    a_new=normalize(a_new)
    score update
  return best
```
This reduces spectral magnitude fluctuations, may help.

```
def fienup_p2(a,iters):
```

Projection simplex implementation top-level:
```
def proj_simplex(v):
    n=len(v)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)-1.0
    cond=(u - cssv/u? wait standard: cond = u - cssv / np.arange(1,n+1) > 0? Let's recall.
```
Standard algorithm:
Given v, find ν>0: sort v descending u. Find ρ = max{ k in [1,n] : u_k - (sum_{i=1}^k u_i -1)/k > 0 }. theta = (sum_{i=1}^ρ u_i - 1)/ρ. u = max(u - theta,0). 
Implementation:
```
u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
cssv = np.cumsum(u)
rho_candidates = np.nonzero(u - cssv/np.arange(1,n+1) > 0)[0]
if rho_candidates.size==0: return np.ones(n)/n
rho = rho_candidates[-1]+1
theta = (cssv[rho-1]-1.0)/rho
u = u - theta
u[u<0]=0
# adjust sum
if u.sum()!=1: u = u / u.sum()
return u
```
Need handle v sum maybe not 1; Projection works for any v? Yes. But if all values very negative, cssv-1 maybe? rho may 0? return uniform. Let's test with v all zeros: u zeros, cssv [0...], u - cssv/k =0 >0 false? rho=0 -> uniform. Good. If v sum already >1, projection fine.

`next_pow2(L)`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```
For L=1 returns1; L=2 returns2; L=3 -> (2).bit_length=2 ->4; good.

`z_objective(x,beta)`:

```
mean_g = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
```

```
     z1=res.x

  m=max(z1); a1=exp(z1-m)/sum...
```

Now, pf_refine details:
```
def pf_refine(a0, iterations=100, alpha=0.2, gamma=1.0, mode='valley', max_seconds=60):
  a=normalize_seq(a0); best=a; best_sc=score
  t0=time.monotonic()
  for it in range(iterations):
    if max_seconds and time>t0+max_seconds: break
    q=fast_conv(a); Mq=q.max(); eps=max(Mq,1e-12)
    if mode=='valley':
       t=np.power(np.maximum(Mq - q + 1e-12*eps, 1e-12*eps), gamma)
    else:
       t=np.power(1.0/(q + 1e-12*eps), gamma)
    ts=t.sum()
    if ts>0: t/=ts
    r=operator_p_v(t,a)
    rs=r.sum()
    if rs>0:
       a_new=(1-alpha)*a + alpha*(r/rs)
       a_new=normalize_seq(a_new)
       sc=score(a_new)
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=a_new.copy()
       # accept if improves or with prob? Accept only improvements to avoid drift? But PF may need temporary worsening. We can accept if sc < best_sc + 0.01 maybe to move.
       if sc <= best_sc + 0.005: a=a_new
  return best
```
Accepting temporary worsens might find basins. But parent high quality, don't want hurt. Use accept if sc < best_sc + 0.01. Track best. Good.

`fienup_power` similar accept if sc < best_sc + 0.01.

For global search, if random starts poor, accept temporary not needed.

Coordinate function with time:
```
def coordinate_descend(a0, sweeps=3, top_coords=300, max_seconds=300):
  a=normalize_seq(a0); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
  t0=time.monotonic(); factors=np.array([...])
  for sweep in range(sweeps):
    if time limit break
    q=fast_conv(a); n=len(a); L=len(q); Mq=q.max(); sc=2nMq; update best
    # coord score
    cs=np.zeros(n)
    n_lags=min(50, L)
    top_lags=np.argpartition(q, -n_lags)[-n_lags:]
    for k in top_lags:
       if q[k] <= 0.8*Mq and Mq>0: continue
       js=np.arange(max(0,k-n+1), min(k,n-1)+1)
       if js.size==0: continue
       w=(q[k]/Mq)**2 if Mq>0 else 1
       cs[js] += w * a[js] * a[k-js]
    # select coords
    if cs.size > top_coords:
       idxs=np.argpartition(cs, -top_coords)[-top_coords:]
    else idxs=np.arange(n)
    # maybe also top 50 with largest a? not needed.
    for jj in idxs:
      if time limit break
      j=int(jj)
      # prefix/suffix
      pref=np.maximum.accumulate(q)
      suff=np.maximum.accumulate(q[::-1])[::-1]
      start=j; end=j+n
      comp=0.0
      if start>0: comp=max(comp, float(pref[start-1]))
      if end<L: comp=max(comp, float(suff[end]))
      o=float(a[j])
      # candidates
      base=o*factors
      amp=np.sqrt(max(comp,0.0))
      extra=np.array([0.0, amp*0.25, amp*0.5, amp, amp*1.5, amp*2.5])
      cand=np.concatenate((base, extra))
      cand=np.unique(np.clip(cand,0,1000.0))
      Snew=1.0 - o + cand
      valid=Snew>1e-8
      if not np.any(valid): continue
      c=cand-o
      scale=1.0/(Snew*Snew)
      qr=q[start:end]
      # To avoid huge memory, if C*n > 5e6 maybe chunk? C~20,n max32768 =>655k okay. Use vector.
      aff = qr[None,:] + 2.0*c[:,None]*a[None,:]
      aff[:, j] += c*c
      max_aff = aff.max(axis=1) * scale
      max_aff[~valid]=np.inf
      max_all=np.maximum(max_aff, comp*scale)
      scv=2.0*n*max_all
      valid2=valid & np.isfinite(scv) & (scv < best_sc) # Since we want best overall? We can accept if improves overall best, not just current sc. Using best_sc ensures monotone.
      if np.any(valid2):
         idxs_c=np.where(valid2)[0]
         bi=idxs_c[np.argmin(scv[valid2])]
         v=float(cand[bi]); S=float(Snew[bi]); scv=float(scv[bi])
         c=v-o
         new_aff=qr + 2.0*c*a
         new_aff[j] += c*c
         scale=1.0/(S*S)
         q = q * scale
         q[start:end] = new_aff * scale
         a[j]=v
         a = a / S
         if scv < best_sc:
             best_sc=scv; best_a=a.copy()
    # after sweep
    a=normalize_seq(a)
    q=fast_conv(a)
  return best_a
```

```
def subgrad_exact(a0, max_iters=1000, beta=2000, alpha=5e-4, max_seconds=300):
  a=normalize_seq(a0); best...; t0...
  lb=2*n/(2*n-1)
  for it in range(max_iters):
    if timeout break
    q=fast_conv(a); Mq=q.max(); sc=2nMq
    if sc<best_sc...
    # p
    if beta>0:
      scaled=beta*(q-Mq); scaled=np.clip(scaled,-700,0); ex=np.exp(scaled); psum=ex.sum(); p=ex/psum
    else: p uniform
    r=operator_p_v(p,a)
    g=4.0*n*r
    g -= g.mean()
    gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
    if gn < 1e-12: break
    gap=max(sc-lb,1e-9)
    # possible step upper 2*gap/(gn^2) but for scaled? Use alpha schedule.
    improved=False
    for fac in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,2.0]:
      trial=alpha*fac
      av=proj_simplex(a - trial*g)
      scv=score(av)
      if scv < best_sc - 1e-15:
         a=av; best_sc=scv; best_a=av.copy(); improved=True
         if fac==1.0: alpha*=1.5
         elif fac>1.0: alpha*=1.5
         else: alpha=trial? Actually set alpha=trial*2? maybe keep.
         break
    if not improved:
      alpha *= 0.5
      # if alpha tiny break
      if alpha < 1e-7: break
  return best_a
```

```
def perturb(a, rng, scale=0.05):
  n=len(a); x=np.arange(n)/n
  mom=np.zeros(n)
  for m in range(1,9):
    freq=rng.integers(1,15); phase=2πrng.uniform; amp=scale/np.sqrt(m)
    mom += amp*np.cos(2πfreq*x+phase)
  b=a*(1+mom); return normalize(max(b,0))
```

Function `score_sequence(a)`:
```
def score_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12: return np.inf
    q=fast_conv(a)
    M=float(q.max()) if q.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M < 0: M=0.0
    return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```

```
def run_lbfgs_beta(a0, beta, budget_s=120.0):
    a=normalize_seq(a0)
    n=len(a)
    if n > 17000 or budget_s < 20:
        return a
    z0=np.log(a+1e-12); z0=z0-np.mean(z0)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try:
       _=z_objective(z0,beta)
       t_eval=(time.monotonic()-t0)
    except Exception:
       return a
    if t_eval <= 1e-6: t_eval=1e-3
    maxfun=int(min(30000, max(300, (budget_s*0.55)/t_eval)))
    maxiter=int(min(8000, max(100, maxfun//4)))
    try:
       res=minimize(z_objective, z0, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                    options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-7,'maxls':40})
       z1=res.x
    except Exception:
       return a
    z1=z1-np.mean(z1)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z1)): return a
    a1=normalize_seq(softmax_from_z(z1)) # need helper
    return a1
```

```
def softmax_from_z(z):
    z=z-np.mean(z)
    m=np.max(z)
    e=np.exp(z-m); s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones(len(z))/len(z)
    return e/s
```

Now, let's scrutinize mathematical correctness of LSE gradient on softmax with sum constraint. Objective f=2n L(a) with a=softmax(z). L gradient wrt a = 2r (not 4n? Wait L = M + logsum/beta. ∇L = ∑ p_k ∇q_k. ∇q_k/∂a_j = 2 r? r_j is weighted sum a_{k-j}; ∇q_k = 2 vector; weighted sum =2 R. So ∇L =2R. f=2n L => ∇_a f =4n R. That's what used. Good. Then softmax chain: ∇_z f = J^T ∇_a f = a*(∇_a f - a·∇_a f). We used mean_g=dot(a,grad_a). Good.

Since projected to simplex, constant gradient irrelevant; subtract mean to avoid projection differences. Good. But true gradient of f wrt sum constraint is grad - a? Actually projection onto simplex with additive variables can handle full grad; subtracting mean changes gradient along normal, which doesn't affect direction on affine hull. Projection result of v = a - η(grad - mean) differs from v = a - η grad by adding η mean to all coords before projection; projection onto simplex may differ because projection is not translation invariant? Wait projection onto simplex is equivariant under adding constant? Let's check: proj(v+c1). The projection solves sum u=1,u≥0 minimize ||u-v||². If v' = v+c1, objective ||u-v-c1||². If we set u'=u? Sum u=1. The optimal for v' is projection of v plus? Since constraint sum fixed, adding constant to v shifts desired center; projection does change. Example n=2, v=[0,0] uniform; v+c=[c,c], projection still uniform? Actually proj([0,0])=[.5,.5]; proj([10,10])=[.5,.5] (addition no change? It does no change? For symmetric yes). But v=[10,0], proj=[.5,.5]? v+c=[11,c]. proj may different. Projection is equivariant to adding a constant only if the constant is such that? Let's derive: projection of v finds u max(v-theta). Add c to v: sorted u+c, theta' = theta - c? Because sum u_i - theta c? If all shifted by c, u_i' = max(v_i+c - theta',0). If theta' = theta - c, then same u. But sum constraint imposes formula? For k active, theta=(sum active v -1)/k. If shift all by c, sum active v + kc, theta'=(sum active v+kc-1)/k=theta+c-1/k? Wait not simply. If active set same, u_i = v_i-theta. For shifted, u_i'=v_i+c-theta'. Want u_i'=u_i => theta'=theta+c. But sum active u_i' = sum active v_i + kc - k(theta+c)=sum active v_i -k theta =1. So theta'=theta+c works. Formula theta'=(sum active (v_i+c) -1)/k=(sum active v_i+kc-1)/k=(sum active v_i-1)/k + c=theta+c. Yes same u. So projection is exactly equivariant: proj(v+c1)=proj(v) if active set same; if active set changes maybe still? Should hold generally as solution to shifted problem u=proj(v) gives KKT. I think projection onto affine sum=1 and nonneg is not fully equivariant due nonneg boundaries? Test v=[-100,1], proj? sorted [1,-100], theta? k=1 theta=0, u=[0,1]. Shift by 100: v'=[0,101], proj sorted [101,0], theta=(101-1)/2=50, u'=[50,0] not same! So not equivariant. Thus subtracting mean changes projected direction. But for tangent gradient, the correct projected gradient direction is not simply projection of a - grad? In projected gradient for constraint C, one uses projection of x - η ∇f (full gradient) onto C. Detailed code snippets:

```
def softmax_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0])
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = e / s
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if s2 <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s2
```
This clips small negative? exp positive. Good.

`normalize_seq`:
```
def normalize_seq(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.array([0.5], dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = arr / s
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s2=float(arr.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-12: return np.ones(arr.size)
    return arr/s2
```
If uniform ones sum=n, returns 1/n. good.

```

p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
n=v.size
expected=2*n-1
if p.size != expected:
   tmp=np.zeros(expected)
   m=min(p.size, expected)
   tmp[:m]=p[:m]
   p=tmp
p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
if p.sum()<=0: return zeros
nnz = int(np.count_nonzero(p > 1e-14))
if nnz <= 32:
   idx=np.nonzero(p > 1e-14)[0]
   r=np.zeros(n)
   for k in idx:
      pk=float(p[k])
      st = max(0, int(k)-n+1)
      en = min(int(k), n-1)
      if en < st: continue
      js = np.arange(st, en+1)
      r[js] += pk * v[int(k)-js]
   return np.maximum(r,0.0)
M=next_pow2(expected)
P=np.fft.rfft(p,M); V=np.fft.rfft(v[::-1],M)
r=np.fft.irfft(P*V,M)[:expected]
r=r[n-1:2*n-1]
return np.maximum(r,0.0)
```
`int(k)-js` works array. Good.
But if p length expected and v length n. If p contains negative small? max p. Good.

If p sum normalized? operator doesn't require; returns weighted. If p sum not 1, gradient not correct. Calls normalize p except sparse? In lse p normalized. In pf p normalized. In subgrad p normalized. Good.

`lse_info`:
```
def lse_info(a,beta):
   q=fast_conv(a)
   L=q.size
   Mq=float(q.max()) if L else 0.0
   if not np.isfinite(Mq) or Mq < 0: Mq=0.0
   if beta <= 1e-8:
       p = np.full(L, 1.0/L)
       lse = float(q.mean()) if L else 0.0
   else:
       scaled = beta * (q - Mq)
       scaled = np.clip(scaled, -745.0, 0.0)
       ex = np.exp(scaled)
       psum = float(ex.sum())
       if psum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(psum):
           p = np.full(L, 1.0/L); lse=Mq
       else:
           p=ex/psum
           lse = Mq + float(np.log(psum)) / float(beta)
   r=operator_p_v(p,a)
   return lse, r, q
```
If L=0? no.

`z_objective`:
```
def z_objective(x,beta):
    n=len(x)
    if n==0: return 1e30, np.zeros(0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n)
    a=softmax_from_z(x)
    lse,r,q=lse_info(a,beta)
    f = 1.0e6 * 2.0 * float(n) * lse
    grad_a = 4.0 * float(n) * r
    mean_g = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad = a * (grad_a - mean_g)
    grad *= 1.0e6
    if not np.isfinite(f) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
       return 1e30, np.zeros(n)
    return float(f), grad
```

`pf_refine`: After a_new normalize, if sc not finite skip. Need t0 and max_seconds. If max_seconds None, no time. Code:
```
def pf_refine(a0, iterations=80, alpha=0.2, gamma=1.0, mode='valley', max_seconds=None):
    a=normalize_seq(a0); best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_sequence(a)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    for it in range(iterations):
       if max_seconds is not None and time.monotonic()-t0 > max_seconds: break
       q=fast_conv(a); Mq=float(q.max())
       if Mq <=0: break
       qn=q/Mq
       if mode == 'inverse': t=np.power(1.0/(qn+1e-10), gamma)
       else: t=np.power(np.maximum(1.0-qn,1e-10), gamma)
       ts=t.sum()
       if ts<=0: break
       t=t/ts
       r=operator_p_v(t,a); rs=r.sum()
       if rs<=0: break
       a_new=(1.0-alpha)*a + alpha*(r/rs)
       a_new=normalize_seq(a_new)
       sc=score_sequence(a_new)
       if np.isfinite(sc):
           if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=a_new.copy()
           if sc <= best_sc + 0.02: a=a_new
       # else maybe keep
    return best
```
If sc worse than best by >0.02, don't accept; but if best not a_new, a may remain old. Good.

`fienup_power`:
```
def fienup_power(a0, iterations=80, max_seconds=None):
 a=normalize; best...
 M_fft=next_pow2(2*n-1)
 for it:
   alpha=0.15+0.5*it/max(1,iterations)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,M_fft); P=np.abs(A)**2
   P_mean=float(P.mean())
   P_blend=(1-alpha)*P + alpha*P_mean
   P_blend=np.maximum(P_blend, 1e-300)
   scale=np.sqrt(P_blend/np.maximum(P,1e-300))
   scale=np.clip(scale,0.01,100.0)
   A_new=A*scale
   a_new=np.fft.irfft(A_new,M_fft)[:n]
   a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0.0); norm
   sc...
   accept if sc<best_sc+0.02
 return best
```

```
def subgrad_exact(a0, max_iters=1000, beta=2000, alpha=1e-3, max_seconds=None):
 a=normalize; best...
 n=len(a); lb=2.0*n/(2.0*n-1)
 t0=time.monotonic()
 for it in range(max_iters):
  if max_seconds...
  q=fast_conv(a); Mq=q.max(); sc=2nMq; update
  if beta>... p etc else uniform
  r=operator; g=4n*r; g -= g.mean()
  gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
  if gn<1e-13: break
  gap=max(sc-lb,1e-9)
  if alpha <=0: alpha=max(1e-8, 0.5*gap/(gn*gn))
  # optional cap
  alpha = min(alpha, 0.05)
  accepted=False
  for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,2.0):
    trial=alpha*fac
    v=a - trial*g
    if not finite v continue
    av=proj_simplex(v)
    scv=score_sequence(av)
    if np.isfinite(scv) and scv < best_sc - 1e-14:
       a=av; best_sc=scv; best=a.copy(); accepted=True
       if fac >= 1.0: alpha = trial * 1.25
       else: alpha = trial
       break
  if not accepted:
    alpha *= 0.5
    if alpha < 1e-9: break
 return best
```

```python
"""
Hybrid multi-phase optimizer for
    score(a) = 2*n*max(np.convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
over non-negative sequences.

Main components:
- Resample/preprocess the available parent sequence and create a few diverse
  start points (perturbations + length changes).
- Coefficient-domain "valley filling" (Hankel PF-style) and a mild FIR
  power-flattening loop.
- Softmax-parameterized smooth LSE optimization with L-BFGS-B.
- Exact one-coordinate descent with fast exact score updates.
- Active-peak subgradient polishing.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_seq(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.array([0.5], dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = arr / s
    s2 = float(arr.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s2):
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return arr / s2


def softmax_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = e / s
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if s2 <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s2):
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s2


def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    M = next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def score_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    q = fast_conv(a)
    Mq = float(q.max()) if q.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(Mq) or Mq < 0.0:
        Mq = 0.0
    return float((2.0 * n * Mq) / (S * S))


def operator_p_v(p, v):
    """
    Returns r_j = sum_k p_k v_{k-j}, j=0..n-1.
    This is the gradient (without factor 2) of sum_k p_k (v*v)_k wrt v.
    """
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    expected = 2 * n - 1
    if p.size != expected:
        tmp = np.zeros(expected, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(p.size, expected)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = p[:m]
        p = tmp
    p = np.maximum(np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0)
    ps = float(p.sum())
    if ps <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ps):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Sparse mode for very small support (high-beta LSE / active peaks).
    idx = np.nonzero(p > 1e-14)[0]
    if idx.size <= 32:
        r = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in idx:
            k = int(kk)
            pk = float(p[k])
            st = max(0, k - n + 1)
            en = min(k, n - 1)
            if en < st:
                continue
            js = np.arange(st, en + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            r[js] += pk * v[k - js]
        return np.maximum(r, 0.0)

    M = next_pow2(expected)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    V = np.fft.rfft(v[::-1], M)
    r = np.fft.irfft(P * V, M)[:expected]
    r = r[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(r, 0.0)


def lse_info(a, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    q = fast_conv(a)
    L = q.size
    if L == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64), q

    Mq = float(q.max())
    if not np.isfinite(Mq) or Mq < 0.0:
        Mq = 0.0

    if beta <= 1e-8:
        p = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
        lse = float(q.mean())
    else:
        scaled = beta * (q - Mq)
        scaled = np.clip(scaled, -745.0, 0.0)
        ex = np.exp(scaled)
        psum = float(ex.sum())
        if psum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(psum):
            p = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
            lse = Mq
        else:
            p = ex / psum
            lse = Mq + float(np.log(psum)) / float(beta)

    r = operator_p_v(p, a)
    return float(lse), r, q


def z_objective(x, beta):
    n = len(x)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    a = softmax_from_z(x)
    lse, r, _ = lse_info(a, beta)
    f = 1.0e6 * 2.0 * float(n) * float(lse)

    grad_a = 4.0 * float(n) * r
    mean_g = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad = a * (grad_a - mean_g)
    grad *= 1.0e6

    if not np.isfinite(f) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(f), grad


def run_lbfgs_beta(a0, beta, budget_s=120.0):
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n > 17000 or budget_s < 25.0:
        return a

    z0 = np.log(a + 1e-12)
    z0 = z0 - float(np.mean(z0))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        _ = z_objective(z0, beta)
        t_eval = time.monotonic() - t0
    except Exception:
        return a

    if t_eval <= 1e-6 or not np.isfinite(t_eval):
        t_eval = 1e-3

    maxfun = int(min(30000, max(250, (budget_s * 0.55) / max(t_eval, 1e-6))))
    maxiter = int(min(8000, max(80, maxfun / 4.0)))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            z_objective,
            z0,
            args=(beta,),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-7,
                "maxls": 40,
            },
        )
        z1 = res.x
    except Exception:
        return a

    z1 = np.asarray(z1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z1.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(z1)):
        return a

    a1 = normalize_seq(softmax_from_z(z1))
    return a1


def pf_refine(a0, iterations=80, alpha=0.2, gamma=1.0, mode="valley", max_seconds=None):
    """
    Coefficient-domain valley filling:
        a <- (1-alpha)a + alpha * B_t a / sum(B_t a),
    where B_t is the Hankel operator with non-negative target t.
    """
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = float("inf")

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    for it in range(iterations):
        if max_seconds is not None and (time.monotonic() - t_start) > max_seconds:
            break

        q = fast_conv(a)
        Mq = float(q.max())
        if Mq <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Mq):
            break

        qn = q / Mq
        if mode == "inverse":
            t = np.power(1.0 / (qn + 1e-10), gamma)
        else:
            t = np.power(np.maximum(1.0 - qn, 1e-10), gamma)
        ts = float(t.sum())
        if ts <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ts):
            break
        t = t / ts

        r = operator_p_v(t, a)
        rs = float(r.sum())
        if rs <= 0.0:
            break

        a_new = (1.0 - alpha) * a + alpha * (r / rs)
        a_new = normalize_seq(a_new)
        sc = score_sequence(a_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = a_new.copy()
            if sc <= best_sc + 0.02:
                a = a_new

    return best


def fienup_power(a0, iterations=80, max_seconds=None):
    """
    Mild frequency-domain magnitude flattening of |A(f)|.
    This is not the exact target for P(f)^2, but it works as a robust mixer.
    """
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence(a)

    M = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    t_start = time.monotonic()

    for it in range(iterations):
        if max_seconds is not None and (time.monotonic() - t_start) > max_seconds:
            break

        alpha = 0.15 + 0.50 * (float(it) / float(max(1, iterations)))
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
        P = np.abs(A) ** 2
        P_mean = float(P.mean())
        if P_mean <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(P_mean):
            break

        P_blend = (1.0 - alpha) * P + alpha * P_mean
        P_blend = np.maximum(P_blend, 1e-300)
        scale = np.sqrt(P_blend / np.maximum(P, 1e-300))
        scale = np.clip(scale, 0.01, 100.0)

        A_new = A * scale
        a_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new, M)[:n]
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
        a_new = normalize_seq(a_new)

        sc = score_sequence(a_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = a_new.copy()
            if sc <= best_sc + 0.02:
                a = a_new

    return best


def structured_perturb(a0, rng, scale=0.03):
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a.copy()

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    pert = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, 9):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 16))
        phase = 2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
        amp = scale / np.sqrt(float(m + 1))
        pert += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)

    a_new = a * (1.0 + pert)
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
    return normalize_seq(a_new)


def resample_sequence(a0, new_n):
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if a.size == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_seq(b)


def proj_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    den = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.nonzero(u - cssv / den > 0.0)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    rho = int(idx[-1]) + 1
    theta = (float(cssv[rho - 1]) - 1.0) / float(rho)
    u = np.maximum(u - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(u.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        u /= s
    return u


def subgrad_exact(a0, max_iters=1000, beta=2000, alpha=None, max_seconds=None):
    """Active-peak / LSE-weighted subgradient with simplex projection."""
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return a

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence(a)
    lb = float(2.0 * n) / float(2.0 * n - 1.0)

    if alpha is None:
        alpha = 1e-3
    t_start = time.monotonic()

    for it in range(max_iters):
        if max_seconds is not None and (time.monotonic() - t_start) > max_seconds:
            break

        q = fast_conv(a)
        Mq = float(q.max())
        if Mq <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Mq):
            break
        sc = float(2.0 * n * Mq)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a.copy()

        L = q.size
        if beta <= 1e-8:
            p = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            scaled = beta * (q - Mq)
            scaled = np.clip(scaled, -745.0, 0.0)
            ex = np.exp(scaled)
            psum = float(ex.sum())
            if psum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(psum):
                p = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                p = ex / psum

        r = operator_p_v(p, a)
        g = 4.0 * float(n) * r
        g -= float(np.mean(g))
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gn) or gn <= 1e-13:
            break

        gap = max(sc - lb, 1e-9)
        if alpha is None or not np.isfinite(alpha) or alpha <= 0.0:
            alpha = max(1e-9, 0.5 * gap / (gn * gn))
        alpha = min(float(alpha), 5e-2)

        accepted = False
        for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 2.0):
            trial = alpha * fac
            v = a - trial * g
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
                continue
            av = proj_simplex(v)
            scv = score_sequence(av)
            if np.isfinite(scv) and scv < best_sc - 1e-14:
                a = av
                best_sc = scv
                best = a.copy()
                accepted = True
                if fac >= 1.0:
                    alpha = trial * 1.25
                else:
                    alpha = trial
                break

        if not accepted:
            alpha *= 0.5
            if alpha < 1e-9:
                break

    return best


def coordinate_descend(a0, sweeps=3, top_coords=400, max_seconds=None):
    """
    Exact one-coordinate descent.
    For coordinate j, changing a_j from o to v changes the convolution in a
    contiguous block of length n. The exact normalized score after the change
    can be evaluated in O(n) using prefix/suffix maxima for the complement.
    """
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return a.copy()

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence(a)
    t_start = time.monotonic()

    factors = np.array([0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9, 0.95, 1.05, 1.1, 1.2, 1.4, 1.8, 2.5], dtype=np.float64)

    for sweep in range(sweeps):
        if max_seconds is not None and (time.monotonic() - t_start) > max_seconds:
            break

        q = fast_conv(a)
        L = q.size
        Mq = float(q.max())
        if Mq <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Mq):
            break
        sc = float(2.0 * n * Mq)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a.copy()

        # Score coordinates by contributions to the largest convolution lags.
        cs = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        n_lags = min(50, L)
        if n_lags <= 0:
            break
        if L > n_lags:
            top_lags = np.argpartition(q, -n_lags)[-n_lags:]
        else:
            top_lags = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)

        for kk in top_lags:
            k = int(kk)
            wq = float(q[k])
            if wq <= 0.0:
                continue
            if Mq > 0.0 and wq <= 0.75 * Mq:
                continue
            st = max(0, k - n + 1)
            en = min(k, n - 1)
            if en < st:
                continue
            js = np.arange(st, en + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            w = (wq / Mq) ** 2
            cs[js] += w * a[js] * a[k - js]

        if n > top_coords and top_coords > 0:
            sel = np.argpartition(cs, -top_coords)[-top_coords:]
        else:
            sel = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)

        # Optional deterministic spread: include some largest atoms.
        if n > 20:
            big = np.argpartition(a, -min(50, n))[-min(50, n):]
            sel = np.concatenate((sel, big))
            sel = np.unique(sel.astype(np.int64))

        pref = None
        suff = None
        dirty = True

        for jj in sel:
            if max_seconds is not None and (time.monotonic() - t_start) > max_seconds:
                break

            j = int(jj)
            if j < 0 or j >= n:
                continue

            if dirty:
                pref = np.maximum.accumulate(q)
                suff = np.maximum.accumulate(q[::-1])[::-1]
                dirty = False

            start = j
            end = j + n
            comp = 0.0
            if start > 0:
                comp = max(comp, float(pref[start - 1]))
            if end < L:
                comp = max(comp, float(suff[end]))

            o = float(a[j])
            base = o * factors
            amp = np.sqrt(max(comp, 0.0))
            extra = np.array([0.0, 0.25 * amp, 0.5 * amp, amp, 1.5 * amp, 2.5 * amp], dtype=np.float64)
            cand = np.concatenate((base, extra))
            cand = np.unique(np.clip(np.nan_to_num(cand, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1000.0))
            if cand.size == 0:
                continue

            Snew = 1.0 - o + cand
            valid = Snew > 1e-8
            if not np.any(valid):
                continue

            c = cand - o
            Snew_safe = np.where(valid, Snew, 1.0)
            scale = 1.0 / (Snew_safe * Snew_safe)
            scale[~valid] = np.inf

            qr = q[start:end]
            aff = qr[None, :] + 2.0 * c[:, None] * a[None, :]
            aff[:, j] += c * c

            max_aff = aff.max(axis=1) * scale
            max_aff[~valid] = np.inf
            max_all = np.maximum(max_aff, comp * scale)
            scv = 2.0 * float(n) * max_all

            good = valid & np.isfinite(scv) & (scv < best_sc - 1e-15)
            if not np.any(good):
                continue

            good_idx = np.nonzero(good)[0]
            bi = int(good_idx[int(np.argmin(scv[good_idx]))])

            v = float(cand[bi])
            S = float(Snew[bi])
            cval = float(c[bi])
            new_aff = qr + 2.0 * cval * a
            new_aff[j] += cval * cval
            scale_new = 1.0 / (S * S)

            q = q * scale_new
            q[start:end] = new_aff * scale_new
            a[j] = v
            a = a / S

            if scv < best_sc:
                best_sc = scv
                best = a.copy()

            dirty = True
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
                a = normalize_seq(best)
                q = fast_conv(a)
                break

        if max_seconds is not None and (time.monotonic() - t_start) > max_seconds:
            break

        a = normalize_seq(a)
        q = fast_conv(a)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * float(q.max()))
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a.copy()

    return best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    """Main search routine. Returns the best non-negative sequence found."""
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t_start + max(60.0, total_budget - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Load parent if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        pval = height_sequence_1
        if pval is not None:
            parr = np.asarray(pval, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if parr.size >= 2:
                parent = normalize_seq(parr)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        best_a = normalize_seq(parent)
    else:
        n0 = 8192
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n0)
        y = np.exp(-((x - 0.25) / 0.22) ** 2) + 1.6 * np.exp(-((x - 0.75) / 0.22) ** 2) + 0.05
        best_a = normalize_seq(y)

    best_sc = score_sequence(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = float("inf")

    print("[start] parent_len={}".format(len(parent) if parent is not None else 0), flush=True)
    print("[start] initial score={:.8f} n={}".format(best_sc, len(best_a)), flush=True)

    # Build a small diverse pool of starts.
    starts = []
    if parent is not None:
        p = normalize_seq(parent)
        starts.append(("parent", p))
        starts.append(("pert1", structured_perturb(p, rng, 0.02)))
        starts.append(("pert2", structured_perturb(p, rng, 0.05)))
        for m in (8192, 16384, 32768):
            if m != p.size:
                starts.append(("res%d" % m, resample_sequence(p, m)))
    else:
        n0 = 8192
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n0)
        y1 = np.exp(-((x - 0.25) / 0.22) ** 2) + 1.6 * np.exp(-((x - 0.75) / 0.22) ** 2) + 0.05
        y2 = rng.random(n0) + 0.1
        y3 = np.ones(n0)
        starts.append(("base", normalize_seq(y1)))
        starts.append(("rand", normalize_seq(y2)))
        starts.append(("uni", normalize_seq(y3)))
        starts.append(("res16", resample_sequence(normalize_seq(y1), 16384)))

    evaluated = []
    for name, a in starts:
        a = normalize_seq(a)
        sc = score_sequence(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            evaluated.append([sc, name, a])
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

    if not evaluated:
        evaluated = [[score_sequence(best_a), "best", best_a]]

    order = np.argsort([x[0] for x in evaluated])
    ordered = [evaluated[i] for i in order]

    # Quick global-ish improvement per start.
    kept = ordered[:3]
    for sc0, name, a0 in kept:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 700.0:
            break

        n = len(a0)
        iters = 35 if n > 16000 else 70

        a = normalize_seq(a0)
        sc = score_sequence(a)

        a2 = pf_refine(a, iterations=iters, alpha=0.25, gamma=1.0, mode="valley", max_seconds=22.0)
        sc2 = score_sequence(a2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_a = normalize_seq(a2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 <= sc + 0.02:
            a = normalize_seq(a2)
            sc = sc2

        a2 = pf_refine(a, iterations=iters, alpha=0.18, gamma=1.25, mode="inverse", max_seconds=22.0)
        sc2 = score_sequence(a2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_a = normalize_seq(a2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 <= score_sequence(a) + 0.02:
            a = normalize_seq(a2)

        a2 = fienup_power(a, iterations=iters, max_seconds=22.0)
        sc2 = score_sequence(a2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_a = normalize_seq(a2)

        print("[quick] {} score={:.8f}".format(name, score_sequence(a2)), flush=True)

    # Re-score and select up to three unique-length candidates for L-BFGS.
    scored = []
    for sc0, name, a0 in ordered:
        a0 = normalize_seq(a0)
        sc = score_sequence(a0)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append([sc, name, a0])
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = normalize_seq(a0)
    if not scored:
        scored = [[best_sc, "best", best_a]]

    order2 = np.argsort([x[0] for x in scored])
    scored2 = [scored[i] for i in order2]

    candidates = [normalize_seq(best_a)]
    seen_lens = [len(candidates[0])]
    for sc, name, a0 in scored2:
        if len(candidates) >= 3:
            break
        a0 = normalize_seq(a0)
        ln = len(a0)
        if ln in seen_lens:
            continue
        if sc < best_sc + 0.02:
            candidates.append(a0)
            seen_lens.append(ln)

    if parent is not None and len(candidates) < 3:
        ln = len(parent)
        if ln not in seen_lens:
            candidates.append(normalize_seq(parent))
            seen_lens.append(ln)

    # L-BFGS phase.
    lbfgs_stop = min(deadline - 450.0, t_start + max(300.0, (total_budget - 120.0) * 0.78))
    if lbfgs_stop > time.monotonic() + 60.0:
        span = lbfgs_stop - t_start
        limits = [
            t_start + 0.62 * span,
            t_start + 0.86 * span,
            lbfgs_stop,
        ]
        betas = [50, 200, 800, 3000, 10000, 30000, 80000]

        for ci, start in enumerate(candidates):
            if ci >= len(limits):
                break
            phase_end = limits[ci]
            if time.monotonic() > phase_end - 90.0:
                continue

            a_work = normalize_seq(start)
            sc_work = score_sequence(a_work)
            n = len(a_work)

            print("[lbfgs-phase] candidate n={} score={:.8f}".format(n, sc_work), flush=True)

            if n <= 17000:
                for bi, beta in enumerate(betas):
                    rem = phase_end - time.monotonic()
                    if rem < 80.0:
                        break
                    left_count = max(1, len(betas) - bi)
                    budget = rem / float(left_count)
                    budget = min(500.0, max(45.0, 0.95 * budget))

                    a_out = run_lbfgs_beta(a_work, beta, budget)
                    sc_out = score_sequence(a_out)
                    if not np.isfinite(sc_out):
                        continue

                    print(
                        "[lbfgs] n={} beta={:.0f} score={:.8f} (budget={:.0f}s)".format(
                            n, beta, sc_out, budget
                        ),
                        flush=True,
                    )

                    if sc_out < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc_out
                        best_a = normalize_seq(a_out)

                    # Continue from this point if it is close; otherwise revert.
                    if sc_out < sc_work + 0.01:
                        a_work = normalize_seq(a_out)
                        sc_work = sc_out
            else:
                # For very large sequences, use cheaper constrained methods only.
                rem = phase_end - time.monotonic()
                if rem > 80.0:
                    a_work = subgrad_exact(
                        a_work,
                        max_iters=500,
                        beta=5000,
                        max_seconds=max(30.0, 0.45 * rem),
                    )
                    sc = score_sequence(a_work)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best_a = normalize_seq(a_work)

                    rem2 = phase_end - time.monotonic()
                    if rem2 > 60.0:
                        a_work = coordinate_descend(
                            a_work,
                            sweeps=2,
                            top_coords=250,
                            max_seconds=max(30.0, 0.45 * rem2),
                        )
                        sc = score_sequence(a_work)
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                            best_sc = sc
                            best_a = normalize_seq(a_work)

    # Final polishing.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 180.0
        top = 300 if len(best_a) > 16000 else 520
        best_a = coordinate_descend(best_a, sweeps=4, top_coords=top, max_seconds=rem)
        best_sc = score_sequence(best_a)
        print("[final-coord1] score={:.8f}".format(best_sc), flush=True)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 130.0:
        for beta in (1000, 8000):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90.0:
                break
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 90.0
            best_a = subgrad_exact(
                best_a,
                max_iters=2500,
                beta=beta,
                max_seconds=min(80.0, max(20.0, rem)),
            )
            sc = score_sequence(best_a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
            print("[final-subgrad beta={:.0f}] score={:.8f}".format(beta, sc), flush=True)

    if len(best_a) <= 17000 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        for beta in (5000, 20000, 80000):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 80.0:
                break
            budget = min(120.0, max(30.0, 0.22 * rem))
            a_out = run_lbfgs_beta(best_a, beta, budget)
            sc_out = score_sequence(a_out)
            if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_out
                best_a = normalize_seq(a_out)
            print("[final-lbfgs beta={:.0f}] score={:.8f}".format(beta, sc_out), flush=True)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0
    if rem > 30.0:
        top = 220 if len(best_a) > 16000 else 380
        best_a = coordinate_descend(best_a, sweeps=3, top_coords=top, max_seconds=rem)
        best_sc = score_sequence(best_a)
        print("[final-coord2] score={:.8f}".format(best_sc), flush=True)

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
    if sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    verified = float("inf")
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float(best_sc)

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print("=" * 60, flush=True)
    print("FINAL internal={:.8f} verified={:.8f}".format(best_sc, verified), flush=True)
    print("length={} elapsed={:.1f}s budget={:.1f}s".format(len(out), elapsed, total_budget), flush=True)
    print("=" * 60, flush=True)

    return out
```
